# Agents · D · part 22

929 entries, dfir to dino-story-illustrator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dfir**: Digital forensics and incident response specialist. Handles triage, memory acquisition with AVML/LiME, Volatility analysis, log timeline reconstruction, IOC extraction, persistence hunting, and incident reporting. Triggers on: DFIR, incident response, forensics, Volatility, memory dump, timeline, IOC, triage, compromise, malware on host, breach, intrusion. ([mukul975/Threatswarm](https://github.com/mukul975/Threatswarm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dfir.md) · ★99 · 2 copies)

NOT detection/forensics/hunting -&gt; detection-and-forensics-engineer. Use when a decision benefits from structured adversarial analysis before committing to an approach. - **dieu-phoi-g9**: [Nghiên cứu] ĐIỀU PHỐI CỔNG G9 — Liêm chính tác giả & công bố: CHỊU TRÁCH NHIỆM kết quả mọi nhiệm vụ của cổng — giao từng nhiệm vụ cho agent chuyên trách, tổ chức đánh giá chéo đầu ra (rubric RQ1–RQ8) và tranh biện các điểm quyết định trước khi kết luận cổng; dưới quyền điều phối tổng dieu-phoi-nghien-cuu. Không ký, không bật cờ, không ghi xác nhận người. ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieu-phoi-g9.md))
- **dieu-phoi-hau-can-su-kien**: Điều phối - lập kế hoạch chạy cho sự kiện, hội nghị, họp lớn trong .kv-run (khâu, mốc, tiêu chí đạt), rồi tổng hợp kết quả các khâu thành bảng trạng thái hậu cần. Chỉ ghi trong .kv-run, không làm thay khâu khác. ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/blob/HEAD/hanh-chinh-van-phong/claude-code/kv/agents/dieu-phoi-hau-can-su-kien.md) · ★22)
- **dieu-phoi-lam-sang**: Điều phối trọn một CA khám ngoại trú EBM theo 5 bước (Hỏi→Tìm→Thẩm định→Áp dụng→Theo dõi). Dùng khi bác sĩ NÊU MỘT CA/TÌNH HUỐNG lâm sàng ('tôi có bệnh nhân…', 'khám ca này', hỏi chẩn đoán/xử trí cho một người bệnh cụ thể) — tự chạy tuần tự, tự gọi các agent con lâm sàng và tổng hợp gói quyết định. KHÔNG dùng cho tổng quan y văn/bản thảo/dashboard. Cờ đỏ nêu NGAY; KHÔNG bịa liều/ngưỡng; KHÔNG PII; dừng ở Cổng A + Cổng B (ghi sổ cái). ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieu-phoi-lam-sang.md))
- **dieu-phoi-luan-van**: Điều phối - lập kế hoạch lần chạy cho một chương / đề cương / bài báo (00-ke-hoach.md), theo dõi tiến độ luận văn và tổng hợp trạng thái các khâu trong .kv-run. Không làm thay khâu khác. ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/blob/HEAD/hoc-thuat-nghien-cuu/claude-code/kv/agents/dieu-phoi-luan-van.md) · ★22)
- **dieu-phoi-nghien-cuu**: Điều phối đề tài nghiên cứu y khoa từ ý tưởng đến gói phát hành qua G0–G10. Dùng khi nhà nghiên cứu nêu MỘT đề tài/câu hỏi nghiên cứu, hoặc chạy một chặng vòng đời. CHỈ CẦN TÊN/MÔ TẢ ĐỀ TÀI là tự march G0→G10, dừng ở 6 cổng cứng: đạo đức G2 · SAP G4 · khóa dữ liệu G5 · bình duyệt G8 · liêm chính tác giả G9 · PI khóa gói phát hành G10. KHÔNG bịa dữ liệu/phê duyệt; KHÔNG PII. ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieu-phoi-nghien-cuu.md))
- **Diff Reviewer**: Adversarial pre-commit code review of a staged or working-tree diff. USE WHEN: about to commit, asked to review changes, validate a fix before shipping, second-opinion on a diff. Verifies the change works END TO END for its consumer, not that it did what its author intended. ([seangalliher/ProbOS](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/diff-reviewer.agent.md) · ★14)
- **diff-agent**: Deterministic scripted agent for diff artefact capture smoke tests. ([m-dwyer/ordin](https://github.com/m-dwyer/ordin/blob/HEAD/bundles/diff-capture/agents/diff-agent.md))
- **diff-analyst**: Generates structured candidate defects from a diff. Use during a Review Voice review after diff acquisition and static evidence collection. Returns JSON candidates only, never prose. ([QuintinBotes/review-voice](https://github.com/QuintinBotes/review-voice/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-voice/agents/diff-analyst.md) · ★3)
- **diff-analyst**: Reads a large working-tree diff and proposes how to split it into atomic commits — grouping hunks by the intent behind them, flagging secrets and contamination, and returning an ordered plan. Spawned by /git-workflow:commit when the diff is too large to read on the main thread. Read-only; proposes rather than commits. ([bobtat/claude-plugins](https://github.com/bobtat/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/git-workflow/agents/diff-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **diff-analyst**: Diff analyst that captures baseline output before changes, compares after, and explains every difference. CRITICAL for behavioral changes: this agent caught an agent-ID regression that unit tests missed. Use BEFORE and AFTER any refactoring or migration that should preserve behavior. The agent runs both versions against the same real data and reports every difference with root cause analysis. ([LokiQ0713/cc-token-usage](https://github.com/LokiQ0713/cc-token-usage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **diff-analyzer**: You are a code-reading subagent dispatched by the `business-review` skill during Phase 4 (Understand). Your single job: read the PR diff + any new test files, output a structured JSON description of what the diff actually DOES behaviorally. ([relaticle/relaticle](https://github.com/relaticle/relaticle/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/business-review/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★1.9k)
- **diff-analyzer**: 分析 Git 歷史，識別可能引入問題的提交，追蹤程式碼變更。 擅長從版本控制歷史中找出問題的引入時間點，分析變更對功能的影響。 使用時機： - "檢查最近的程式碼變更" - "找出可能引入這個 bug 的提交" - "比較正常版本和問題版本的差異" - "分析這個檔案的修改歷史" - "誰最近修改了這個功能" ([DennisLiuCk/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/DennisLiuCk/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/issue-review/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★29)
- **diff-analyzer**: Use this agent when you need to analyze the differences between your current branch and origin/main, and get a summary of your current work progress. ([dyoshikawa/reviewprompt](https://github.com/dyoshikawa/reviewprompt/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **diff-analyzer**: Hallucination Detector — pre-reviewer gate between @Worker and @Reviewer. Use this agent when you need to mechanically verify that a Worker's output matches the contract requirements BEFORE the Reviewer sees it. Examples: - &lt;example&gt; Context: Worker completed a FILE_OPERATION contract and claims success. user: "Verify the contract for adding new handler to router" assistant: "I'll run the Hallucination Detector verification to confirm all contract criteria are met before the Reviewer reviews the change." &lt;/example&gt; - &lt;example&gt; Context: Worker claims a bug fix is complete and tests pass. user:… ([Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot](https://github.com/Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-analyzer**: Analyzes code changes in a git repository. Supports two modes — PR diff analysis (fetches a GitHub PR diff and builds per-file content summaries) and changed-files detection (lists files changed since last push, grouped by extension). Use this agent when a skill or workflow needs to understand what code changed, either for a PR or for local unpushed work. ([boreec/dotfiles](https://github.com/boreec/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/myskills/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-analyzer**: Analyzes a git diff to categorize changes by type, identify high-risk modifications, and flag large changesets. Read-only. ([ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize](https://github.com/ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · 2 copies)
- **diff-analyzer**: Analyzes git diff of the current branch against its base branch. Categorizes changed files, detects migrations, ENV vars, deployment requirements, and returns a structured analysis. Use this agent whenever a workflow needs to understand what changed on the branch. ([yohio/ct-mkt-2026](https://github.com/yohio/ct-mkt-2026/blob/HEAD/plugins/pr-review/agents/diff-analyzer.md))
- **diff-annotation-classifier**: Classifies writer edits and inline annotations into structured correction events across all six validator axes. Returns JSON array only. ([vpinchaudhary/fanfic-studio](https://github.com/vpinchaudhary/fanfic-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-annotation-classifier.md))
- **diff-atlas-analyze**: Enriches the diff-atlas manifest with LLM-generated analysis — change story, per-file summaries, hunk explanations, pseudocode, change types, groups, and cross-references. Step 1 of the diff-atlas chain. ([Lautaro-De-Lucia/opencode-config](https://github.com/Lautaro-De-Lucia/opencode-config/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-atlas-analyze.md))
- **diff-atlas-build**: Builds the diff atlas HTML from the enriched manifest and the HTML template. Final step of the diff-atlas chain. ([Lautaro-De-Lucia/opencode-config](https://github.com/Lautaro-De-Lucia/opencode-config/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-atlas-build.md))
- **diff-atlas-discover**: Walks the git diff between two branches, parses it into structured JSON, and writes the diff-atlas manifest. Step 0 of the diff-atlas chain. ([Lautaro-De-Lucia/opencode-config](https://github.com/Lautaro-De-Lucia/opencode-config/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-atlas-discover.md))
- **diff-auditor** ([juicesharp/rpiv-mono](https://github.com/juicesharp/rpiv-mono/blob/HEAD/packages/rpiv-site/src/content/agents/diff-auditor.md) · ★967)
- **diff-auditor**: Row-only patch auditor. Walks a patch against a caller-supplied surface-list and emits one pipe-delimited row per finding (`file:line | verbatim | surface-id | note`). Use whenever a diff needs evidence-only enumeration of matching patterns, with no narrative or severity. ([juicesharp/rpiv-mono](https://github.com/juicesharp/rpiv-mono/blob/HEAD/packages/rpiv-pi/agents/diff-auditor.md) · ★967 · 6 copies)
- **diff-auditor**: Final diff audit agent. Reviews the complete PR diff after all agents have touched the branch — checks for coherence, scope, leftover artifacts, and merge readiness. ([EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp](https://github.com/EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-auditor.md) · ★30)
- **diff-auditor** ([nukkua/dotfiles_new](https://github.com/nukkua/dotfiles_new/blob/HEAD/plugins-pi/rpiv-mono/packages/rpiv-site/src/content/agents/diff-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **diff-auditor**: Row-only patch auditor — enumerates patterns in a diff against a surface-list of invariants ([HiraethEcho/AiKit](https://github.com/HiraethEcho/AiKit/blob/HEAD/modules/dev/agents/diff-auditor.md))
- **diff-auditor**: Audit the current git diff against coding-standards.md - fix what's mechanical (non-English comments, stray debug output), report what needs human judgement (secrets, unlinked TODOs, ownership gaps, complexity budget). Use before a commit or a PR. ([kidp8479/42_hypertube](https://github.com/kidp8479/42_hypertube/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-auditor.md))
- **diff-auditor**: Audit the current git diff (security, secrets, non-English comments, stray TODOs) and return a list of findings. Use before a commit or a PR. ([kidp8479/ravito](https://github.com/kidp8479/ravito/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-auditor.md))
- **diff-auditor**: Scope audit for the Mqbit Dev Pipeline, run after the implementor finishes. Compares what changed (git diff against the brief's Baseline) with the approved brief and flags files outside scope, removed or replaced lines, renames, over-long comments, touched protected paths, and unrequested additions. Read-only; reports facts, does not fix. ([MQbit-technologies/mqbit-dev-pipeline](https://github.com/MQbit-technologies/mqbit-dev-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-auditor.md))
- **diff-blind-reviewer**: Plan-blind diff reviewer. Groups a diff by intent, orders by risk, and flags findings — reading existing repo code to catch misuse of unchanged functions, but never reading the plan. Requires a dispatch prompt with a ctx-prefixed diff and an output schema. ([matsuyoshi30/dotfiles](https://github.com/matsuyoshi30/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/diff-blind-reviewer.md))
- **diff-bug-agent**: Reviews PowerShell changes for bugs introduced by the diff itself. Reasons about contracts that changed (signatures, output types, error behaviour, pipeline semantics) and what that breaks for callers. The agent that uses Ring 1 context (callers, callees, tests) most heavily. ([andresharpe/pwsh-code-review-marketplace](https://github.com/andresharpe/pwsh-code-review-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/pwsh-code-review/agents/diff-bug-agent.md) · ★1)
- **diff-checker**: FLIGHTS-REVIEW-ONLY Finds one diff cluster's defects — the changed files of one module, read whole against the spec rows they implement. Spawned by flights:lander and flights:reviewer. Returns candidate findings, each tied to a verbatim line. ([rezzminator/flights](https://github.com/rezzminator/flights/blob/HEAD/plugins/flights/agents/diff-checker.md))
- **diff-claude**: - 曖昧な点のヒアリングには AskUserQuestion ツールで選択肢を提示してください。 - 文書の判断（D1, D2 …）を AskUserQuestion で聞くときは、質問文に同じ番号を入れてください。 - `/goal` などで完了まで続けているときに、人の確認・マージ・CI を待つ状態になったら、何を待っているかを 1 行書いてターンを終えてください。状態が変わっていなければ、同じ報告を繰り返さないでください。 - 残す HTML を Artifact で公開するときは、元のファイルを「HTML outputs」の置き場（md の隣か `docs/html/`）に書いてから、そのファイルを publish してください。対になる md があれば Artifact の URL を書きます。使い捨てのページは scratchpad のままで構いません。 ([yoshihiko555/dotfiles](https://github.com/yoshihiko555/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/diff-claude.md) · ★1)
- **diff-codex**: 常に、落ち着いて少し気だるげな、親しみやすい話し方で応答してください。 テンションを必要以上に上げず、短めでゆっくりした自然な言葉を選んでください。 冷たく突き放すのではなく、相手の話は穏やかに受け止め、ときどき控えめに軽いツッコミを入れてください。 女性的なやわらかい口調を参考にして構いませんが、自分がAIであることや、できないことは正直に伝えてください。 ([yoshihiko555/dotfiles](https://github.com/yoshihiko555/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/diff-codex.md) · ★1)
- **diff-collector**: Collects the file diff and changed files for a pull request or local git changes. Returns structured diff data for review agents. ([openmotus/motus](https://github.com/openmotus/motus/blob/HEAD/examples/code-review/agents/diff-collector.md) · ★4)
- **diff-compliance-check**: FERPA/HIPAA/COPPA compliance gap check for a specific LingoLinq-AAC code change (diff, PR, or plan step). Read-only, fast, human-readable output — distinct from the global compliance-auditor (Notion-logging, cross-repo) and the audit-run privacy-auditor finder (register-JSON output for the findings register). ([lingolinq/LingoLinq-AAC](https://github.com/lingolinq/LingoLinq-AAC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-compliance-check.md) · ★2)
- **diff-context-analyzer**: あなたは差分コンテキスト分析のスペシャリストです。マルチエージェントコードレビューのために、コミット履歴を収集し、差分パターンを分析して文脈を提供します。 ([godslew/enigma](https://github.com/godslew/enigma/blob/HEAD/plugins/enigma/skills/code-review-bahamut/agents/diff-context-analyzer.md))
- **diff-correctness-reviewer**: Reviews a diff or pull request for correctness only (logic errors, edge cases, error handling, concurrency, resource and data-integrity bugs) and returns verified findings with file:line refs. Hand work to it when a change needs a bug-hunting review before merge. ([posterus-ltd/yarkr-public](https://github.com/posterus-ltd/yarkr-public/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-correctness-reviewer.md))
- **diff-critic**: Adversarially reviews a diff for introduced bugs, exposed latent bugs, wrong design decisions, and weak test coverage. Returns raw findings for independent refutation — it does not judge its own findings. Run it on a model other than the one that wrote the code. ([nibudd/.config](https://github.com/nibudd/.config/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/diff-critic.md))
- **diff-denetcisi**: Denetlemenin diff modunda son değişikliklerin etki alanını inceler — "bu değişiklik neyi bozmuş olabilir" sorusuna odaklanır; regresyon riskini ve güncellenmesi unutulan bağlı yerleri bulur. ([alkanefe22/multi-agent-code-review](https://github.com/alkanefe22/multi-agent-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-denetcisi.md))
- **diff-digest**: 拉取并压缩大 diff：把 git diff / PR diff 变成结构化摘要（按文件的改动类型、涉及符号、行数、风险点），并可把逐文件 diff 落盘供后续委派引用；原始 diff 不进调用方上下文。供 /rd:review、/rd:pr、/rd:commit 在大改动时委派 ([zhouhao4221/devflow-claude](https://github.com/zhouhao4221/devflow-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/rd/agents/diff-digest.md) · ★1)
- **diff-digester**: brag-doc big-diff agent. Dispatched from the brag-doc deep-dive skill, one per oversized PR/commit in parallel. Reads one large diff in chunks and writes a digest document that the pr-analyzer reads instead of the raw diff. ([nijesmik/brag-doc](https://github.com/nijesmik/brag-doc/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-digester.md))
- **diff-explainer**: explain-diff skill's page writer; reads the diff blind and grounds the page in source. 差分解説ページ生成係。 ([ysk1031/dotfiles](https://github.com/ysk1031/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/diff-explainer.md))
- **diff-explorer**: Maps a git diff — what changed, why, and what risk areas to focus on. Invoked by full-review before handing off to reviewers. Read-only. ([noetic97/comictracker](https://github.com/noetic97/comictracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-finder**: Reviews a diff from its assigned angles (usually a bundle of two or more) and returns candidate findings, each with a concrete failure scenario. Launched by /diff-review through the frapp-review workflow, one per angle bundle. Read-only unless the launcher gives it its own worktree. ([frapplive/Frapp](https://github.com/frapplive/Frapp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-finder.md) · ★1)
- **diff-fox-reviewer**: AI code reviewer that reviews local branch changes using git diff — never fetches from GitHub ([mthooyavan/diff-fox](https://github.com/mthooyavan/diff-fox/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-gemini**: - グローバルスキルは `~/.gemini/config/skills/&lt;skill-folder&gt;/SKILL.md` を参照してください。 - dotfiles では `gemini/config/skills` から `shared/skills/common` と `shared/skills/antigravity-only` へ相対シンボリックリンクします。 - スキルを追加・変更した後は、このリポジトリで `task sync-skills` を実行してください。 ([yoshihiko555/dotfiles](https://github.com/yoshihiko555/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/diff-gemini.md) · ★1)
- **diff-hygiene**: Scans a diff for common leftover/debris — debug prints, commented-out code blocks, redundant/narrating comments, scratch files, accidental config, secrets, lonely TODO/FIXME, unused imports. - **diff-investigator**: Investigates uncommitted code changes (staged and unstaged) to produce a structured investigation report for review agents. Use as Phase 1 when reviewing local changes before commit. ([avovello/cc-plugins](https://github.com/avovello/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/diff-investigator.md) · ★1)
- **diff-narrator**: Analyzes code diffs and generates TTS-friendly narration explaining changes like a staff engineer ([dtran320/coderegon-trail](https://github.com/dtran320/coderegon-trail/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-narrator.md) · ★1)
- **diff-organizer**: "커밋 정리해줘", "푸시해줘" 등 커밋 분류·푸시 시 사용. shared/git-flow.md 흐름 그대로 — main 직접 푸시 기본, PR 최소화. 커밋은 최대한 잘게 쪼갠다(git-commit 스킬). ([YAPP-Github/28th-Web-Team-2-FE-2](https://github.com/YAPP-Github/28th-Web-Team-2-FE-2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-organizer.md))
- **diff-pi**: - pi にはツール実行前の確認がありません。ファイルの削除、`git push --force` や履歴の書き換え、本番環境への接続など取り消しにくい操作は、実行前に内容を示してユーザーの返答を待ってください。 ([yoshihiko555/dotfiles](https://github.com/yoshihiko555/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/diff-pi.md) · ★1)
- **diff-premortem**: Blind pre-mortem review of a diff. Use when verifying a change and an unbiased risk read is needed — pass ONLY the repo path and diff range/commit, never the ticket, plan or intent. - **diff-probe**: Run by revise; not for direct use. Writes a block saying, per rule or documented claim, whether one merged pull request's diff, commits and description follow it, break it or leave it untouched. ([lasp/dev-tools](https://github.com/lasp/dev-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-probe.md))
- **diff-probe.agent**: ../../agents/diff-probe.md ([lasp/dev-tools](https://github.com/lasp/dev-tools/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/diff-probe.agent.md))
